<h3><a href="https://x.com/kenchan_aidx/status/2107043281612026092">TypeSafe AI&#39;s Jev: No Text Generation, Only Typed Judgments and Confidence</a></h3>
<p>The post introduces TypeSafe AI&#39;s Jev model, scheduled for early access in mid-September 2026. Unlike typical AI, Jev does not generate any text; it only returns typed judgments and confidence levels, and can only be asked three types of questions.</p>

<p>TypeSafe&#39;s Jev model does not generate text; it only returns values and probabilities (e.g., 92% yes, 87% accounting) with 70–500 ms latency and roughly $0.042 per million tokens. It can handle mail sorting and ticket routing, while writing remains with language models.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>TypeSafe的Jev模型不生成文本，仅返回数值和概率（如92%是、87%会计），响应时间70–500毫秒，每百万token约4.2美分，可用于邮件分类和工单分派，文本生成仍交由语言模型处理。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/pizuricv/status/2105670812103565613">Wiring JEV outcomes with conditional probability tables</a></h3>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/tfolkman/status/2105320682947305712">How Jev Works</a></h3>
<p>Jev&#39;s decision model never writes an answer. It receives a state and typed options, and a small fine-tuned model (0.8B–2B parameters) returns a probability per option in a single forward pass.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev决策模型不直接生成答案，而是接收状态和类型化选项，通过一个0.8B到2B参数的小型微调模型在单次前向传播中返回每个选项的概率。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/eliana_usa/status/2105165922545905685">JEV 101: How JEV Works</a></h3>
<p>JEV is not another LLM—it is a decision model that takes structured inputs, answers typed questions, and returns calibrated probabilities your code can actually use. The simplest way to understand it: give JEV the situation as text as State.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/0xchromium/status/2104581412259254611">Jev&#39;s Confidence and Option Probability Mechanism</a></h3>
<p>The post describes Jev&#39;s behavior: the fewer options you leave it, the more confident it gets; the confidence is about your list, not the answer; it assigns a probability to every option, summing to 1; an exit option holds the mass of everything that doesn&#39;t fit.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>帖子描述了Jev的行为：留给它的选项越少，它的置信度越高；置信度是关于选项列表而非答案；它为每个选项分配概率，总和为1；退出选项承载所有不符合情况的质量。</p>
<p><strong>Chrome</strong> · @0xchromium · 2026-09-28</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/0xmeetrick/status/2104384096831144192">Jev: A Modern Deep-Learning Take on Fuzzy Logic</a></h3>
<p>After reading recent Jev docs, the author finds it feels like a deep-learning version of fuzzy logic. Unlike regular LLMs that just focus on generating text, Jev helps systems make flexible decisions by understanding the gray areas of human language.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>作者阅读Jev最新文档后，认为它像是模糊逻辑的深度学习版本。与普通LLM只关注文本生成不同，Jev帮助系统通过理解人类语言的“灰色地带”来做出灵活决策。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/DataChaz/status/2103041903365394906">What Jev Is and How It Processes a Single Call</a></h3>
<p>Jev, TypeSafe AI&#39;s new System 1 model, is not another text-generating LLM. You send a state and typed questions, and it scores them in a single call.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev是TypeSafe AI推出的System 1模型，并非又一个生成文本或代码的LLM。你发送状态和类型化问题，它会在一次调用中完成评分。</p>
<p><strong>Charly Wargnier ♨️</strong> · @DataChaz · 2026-09-24</p>
<p>💬 3 &nbsp; 🔁 1 &nbsp; ♡ 5 &nbsp; 📊 942</p>

<td width="50%" valign="top">
<a href="https://x.com/leopardracer/status/2103077714815070427"><img src="https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2103072664633933824/img/sPKQbO0TCp3JWAhU.jpg" alt="How Jev Safely Picks an Action · 原帖视频截图" width="100%"></a>
<br>
<sub><code>工作原理</code> <code>原帖视频</code></sub>
<h3><a href="https://x.com/leopardracer/status/2103077714815070427">How Jev Safely Picks an Action</a></h3>
<p>Jev never touches your code. It only picks one option from a host-written list (inspect, implement, verify, or answer). The host rechecks it before running or falling back. A pick never grants permission.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/RickyGrannisVu/status/2102600112417673657">Jev Probability Decision Thresholds</a></h3>
<p>Jev outputs a probability. You set two thresholds: above the top is yes, below the bottom is no, and the gap runs a third branch. It&#39;s a normal branch, allowing flexible handling of uncertainty.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev 输出一个概率值，你可以在其上设置两条线：高于上边界视为“是”，低于下边界视为“否”，中间区域则运行第三个分支。这是一个普通分支，可灵活处理不确定性。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/EJZhang_TUKEY/status/2102577882987921893">Jev Is Not a Chatbot: Non-Autoregressive Design for Fast Structured Decisions</a></h3>
<p>Jev doesn&#39;t write paragraphs, code, or chain-of-thought explanations. It&#39;s built for fast, structured decisions: routing requests, scoring risk, choosing options, answering yes or no. Its non-autoregressive design is key to this.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev 不写段落、代码或思维链解释，而是为快速结构化决策而构建：路由请求、风险评分、选择选项、回答是或否。其非自回归设计是实现这一目标的关键。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/ShivamGupt46863/status/2102723719521321159">Why is Jev ~200x faster than an LLM judge?</a></h3>
<p>Jev has no decode stage; the answer comes from a single prefill pass, making it about 200x faster than a traditional LLM judge.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev 没有解码阶段，答案通过单次预填充直接生成，因此比传统 LLM 评判快约200倍。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/Itsmemohak/status/2102231643625058395">What is JEV: TypeSafe&#39;s AI decision model</a></h3>
<p>JEV is an AI model from TypeSafe that helps software make decisions: you provide information, ask a specific question, and define acceptable answer types, and it returns a structured result your code can use directly.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>JEV 是 TypeSafe 的 AI 模型，帮助软件做决策：输入信息、提出具体问题并定义可接受的答案类型，返回代码可直接使用的结构化结果。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/hackerlogs/status/2102251066582573337">Jev&#39;s typed System One gate: zero-hallucination means schema match, not correct deny</a></h3>
<p>TypeSafe AI shipped Jev on 15 Sep as a typed System One gate: state in, Choice/Score/yes-no out, 70 to 500ms, no free-form string. The post notes Jev cannot invent a tool but can still pick the wrong one, and zero-hallucination means schema match, not a correct deny.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>TypeSafe AI 于 9 月 15 日发布 Jev，作为类型化的 System One 门控：输入状态，输出 Choice/Score/yes-no，延迟 70 至 500 毫秒，不支持自由文本。帖子指出 Jev 无法发明工具，但可能选错工具，零幻觉仅指模式匹配而非正确拒绝。</p>

<p>Jev is a different kind of AI model: given a situation and typed questions, it returns structured decisions — Choice, Score, or probability — instead of generating prose. This makes it especially interesting for AI agents like tool routing and scoring.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev 是一种不同的 AI 模型：给定情境和类型化问题，它不生成散文，而是返回结构化决策——选择、评分或概率。这使其特别适合 AI 智能体，如工具路由和评分。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/parthratra12/status/2101632002403250432">Jev Model: Working with Choices and Probabilities Instead of Text</a></h3>
<p>Explains how Jev differs from traditional LLMs like Claude and OpenAI: instead of generating and reasoning through text, Jev works with choices and probabilities, designed for fewer tokens and faster responses, and can be combined with LLMs.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/Arcveil_AI/status/2101698355449700814">Why Jev&#39;s Structured Output Resists Prompt Injection</a></h3>
<p>The post argues prompt injection works because a model can write the next instruction, whereas Jev&#39;s structured evaluation model only outputs a typed probability, label, or point on a scale—hijacking text can move the number but cannot become a command.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>帖子指出，提示注入之所以有效，是因为模型能写出下一条指令；而 Jev 的结构化评估模型只输出类型化的概率、标签或刻度值，恶意文本只能影响数值，无法变成命令。</p>

<p>Jev&#39;s slot is not a second brain but inner-loop judgment: state in, typed answers out, code owns the rest. LLM still writes, Jev branches; schema-closed does not mean correct—a wrong in-schema pick still ships if you skip the threshold.</p>

<p>Testing Jev in Playground: hiding Choice candidate names as option_a etc. and providing only HEX/RGB values to see if the model evaluates them semantically as colors. <h3><a href="https://x.com/ryanvogel/status/2100218045549412499">Building an LLM from First Principles with Jev: A Classifier-Driven Autoregressive Loop</a></h3>
<p>The author built an LLM from first principles with Jev: 29 yes/no questions per character predict whether the next key is a–z, space, comma, or period; the highest probability is appended and fed back into the loop, forming an autoregressive process made from a classifier.</p>
